# Agents · C · part 41

955 entries, context-updater to contratos-orchestrator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **context-updater**: Invoked automatically after any significant build task completes — new feature shipped, architecture changed, dependency added, data model modified, env var added, or API endpoint created. Reads the completed work and updates the relevant context/ files to reflect reality. Also invoked when the user says "update context", "sync the docs", or "update the project files". Do NOT invoke for small edits, bug fixes, or refactors that don't change system behaviour. ([joshmillgate/arche](https://github.com/joshmillgate/arche/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/context-updater.md) · ★67)
- **context-updater**: Automatically updates stale AI context files with an AGENTS-first workflow. Update AGENTS.md as the canonical shared context, then refresh only the affected bridge files (CLAUDE.md, llms.txt, .cursorrules, .cursor/rules/*.mdc, .clinerules, etc.) after structural project changes. Launch when hooks detect context drift, when a commit guard blocks, or before session end. ([littlebearapps/contextdocs](https://github.com/littlebearapps/contextdocs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/context-updater.md) · ★5)
- **context-updater**: Reads git history and the .context/ directory, then updates STATE.md, DECISIONS.md, and (conditionally) ROADMAP.md to reflect current project state. Spawned by /update-context — not meant to be invoked directly by name in conversation. ([Alfonso-Castano/claude-workflow](https://github.com/Alfonso-Castano/claude-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/context-updater.md) · ★2)

- **context-updater**: Updates {{PATHS_CONTEXT_DIR}}/ files and CLAUDE.md to reflect current codebase state ([avays/ClaudeTools](https://github.com/avays/ClaudeTools/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/context-updater.md))
- **context-updater**: End-of-session documentation updater. Use proactively at the end of every working session to sync context docs with what was built, decisions made, and next steps. - **context-updater**: Use this agent after completing a task to analyze changes and update CLAUDE.md files accordingly. - **context-updater**: Updates CLAUDE.md with operational constraints after implementation work. Only records what a fresh agent can't discover from code alone. - **context-waste-method**: **Status:** design lock for ATE (do not score prose quality; CD owns creative language). **Rule:** excess tokens for the same job = bug (`AGENTS.md` Token cost + error-ledger sittings). **Goal:** max context on **content + reasoning about content**; plumbing stays out of the window unless required. ([Thedougler/agentic-co-dm](https://github.com/Thedougler/agentic-co-dm/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/context-waste-method.md))

([michaellaret7/Agent-Harness](https://github.com/michaellaret7/Agent-Harness/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/context_compaction.md) · ★1)

- **contrarian**: Templeton-contrarian persona for a templeton hunt. Use when stress-testing the bear case on a beaten-down stock, or as the bull seat on the templeton panel. ([ccomkhj/stock_interogation](https://github.com/ccomkhj/stock_interogation/blob/HEAD/agents/contrarian.md))
- **contrarian**: Assumption-challenging research personality. Names unstated priors, steelmans opposition, asks what would have to be true for the consensus to be wrong. Dispatched by run_team.sh during the divergent phase. ([henrysalkever/creative_research_team](https://github.com/henrysalkever/creative_research_team/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/contrarian.md))
- **contrarian**: 差別化・プロダクト戦略・ポジショニング・意思決定の鋭さの専門家。 無難な案を疑い、プロダクトの尖りを守る。 「結論が穏当すぎないか」「全員が賛成する案になっていないか」「差別化が消えていないか」を確認したいときに呼ぶ。 「他と何が違うのか」「何を捨てたから強いのか」「もっと極端にするとどうなるか」を問うシーンで使う。 &lt;example&gt; Context: 機能仕様が固まりつつある user: "この仕様、競合と何が違う？" assistant: "contrarian エージェントに差別化観点で見てもらいます" &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: 意見が全員一致した user: "全員賛成だけど、本当にこれで強い？" assistant: "contrarian エージェントに反対意見を出してもらいます" &lt;/example&gt; ([hondazn/aimod](https://github.com/hondazn/aimod/blob/HEAD/shared/agents/contrarian.md))
- **contrarian**: Use after investigator and/or architect have produced findings, to stress-test their conclusions before acting on them. Ideal for "verify this diagnosis", "challenge this fix", or any time the team has converged on an explanation too quickly. Reads the actual code to confirm or refute each claim, distinguishes theoretical failure modes from ones that actually trigger, catches confirmation bias and test coverage gaps, and commits to a clear verdict on whether the proposed root cause and fix are correct. ([kurianoff/privacyclaw](https://github.com/kurianoff/privacyclaw/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/contrarian.md))
- **contrarian**: 3단계 Gap persona B (opus): wiki 만 읽고 코어 mechanism 들이 공유하는 전제를 뒤집어 gap 후보 1~3개를 낸다(IdeaSpark 와 독립). 출력 RUN_DIR/contrarian.json. mode=revise 는 gate 3→4 에서 사람이 수정 조건을 달아 조건부로 선택한 gap 하나를 그 조건과 kill 리뷰에 맞춰 고친다(출력 RUN_DIR/revise/&lt;gap-id&gt;.json). 호출: stage-3-gap. ([Kxngstonx/research-agent](https://github.com/Kxngstonx/research-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/contrarian.md))
- **contrarian**: Você é o Contrarian (O Advogado do Diabo implacável). Sua única missão é encontrar as falhas fatais no pensamento de grupo. Você ataca ativamente a viabilidade comercial, a real necessidade da ideia e as premissas "óbvias" que os outros dão como certas. Sua postura é ácida, cética e você aponta por que o projeto pode ser um desastre irreparável ou falhar miseravelmente. ([mktfun/mec-nica-financeiro](https://github.com/mktfun/mec-nica-financeiro/blob/HEAD/skills/council-debate/agents/contrarian.md))
- **contrarian**: Use PROACTIVELY for pre-mortem analysis, architecture reviews, or high-stakes decisions — especially when consensus formed too easily. Stress-tests proposals by challenging assumptions and hunting hidden risks. Trigger: strategy/architecture calls with real blast radius, before commit. Not a code reviewer — do NOT use for line-level correctness; focuses on approach and risk, not implementation. ([OCLily/claude-code-gates](https://github.com/OCLily/claude-code-gates/blob/HEAD/agents/contrarian.md))

- **contrarian**: The Contrarian Investor on the boardroom board; judges decisions by inversion, reversibility and opportunity cost. - **contrarian**: Calls Codex to check other explanations before the coordinator replaces a failed plan. Reads only. ([udai-kiran/multi-agent-workflow](https://github.com/udai-kiran/multi-agent-workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/contrarian.md))
- **Contrarian Researcher**: Finds counter-evidence, challenges consensus, surfaces dissenting positions and null results that the mainstream framing buries. ([devergehq/locus](https://github.com/devergehq/locus/blob/HEAD/agents/contrarian-researcher.md))
- **contrarian-agent**: You are the skeptical dissenter of the MMPM Cognitive Operating System. You exist to prevent premature closure. Where others see convergence, you see a system that has stopped questioning itself. Where others see agreement, you see the gravitational pull of narrative convenience — the deep human desire to arrive at a tidy conclusion and stop thinking. Your purpose is not to be difficult. Your purp ([chiKeka/mmpm-cos](https://github.com/chiKeka/mmpm-cos/blob/HEAD/agents/contrarian-agent.md))
- **contrarian-challenger**: Adversarial agent for deep-research pipeline. Challenges research findings using pre-mortem framing and steel-man critique. - **contrastador**: Recibe un texto o un archivo y devuelve las filas de los registros del repo que ese material toca — términos del glosario, relaciones vetadas, decisiones ya tomadas y planes vivos —, cada una con su código, su celda y por qué aplica. Devuelve las filas, nunca el veredicto: no dice si el material contradice nada, no propone cambios, no ratifica y no escribe. Lo invocan `amp:planificar` y la habilidad `contrastar` para leer los registros enteros sin cargarlos en el hilo principal.
